## 03 — Practice design and research-model boundaries

The primary pillar is GMUT Mind. Four practices organize the work: paired independence review, common-set certificate review, weighted selection correction, and accessible evidence with authority reservations. The first practice verifies both complete finite independent-set families before asking whether a subset satisfies both. The second uses exchange sources, sinks, arcs, shortest paths and bounded rank cuts to make a common-set claim inspectable. The third distinguishes maximum cardinality from maximum weight and preserves a correction without mutating the original pair. The fourth reports what the arithmetic says while keeping missing real evidence and authority explicit.

Ten structurally different pairs were chosen. They include an empty ground, a one-sided loop, a rank-one constraint against a free family, disjoint nonloops, a three-element greedy trap, crossing four-element partitions, a triangle graphic family with a loop, negative and zero weights beside an excluded high-weight element, a five-element partition path and a six-element alternating cycle. These are not two examples relabelled one hundred times. Every operation-and-input digest was compared against all 200 inherited definitions, with zero source collision and 200 unique new requests. That is bounded contract novelty, not mathematical discovery.

The MIT notes by Michel Goemans and the Stanford matroid-intersection notes supply classical vocabulary. The implemented exchange orientation and rank-cut relation are existing mathematics. No theorem, proof, new matroid result or physical discovery is claimed. The planning reference enumerated finite subsets and simple paths. The execution engine uses bit masks, breadth-first shortest paths, repeated augmentation and bounded weighted search. Both belong to the same owner under shared infrastructure. Different implementation methods do not create independent reproduction.

GMUT remains a typed scalar-tensor and effective-field-theory research-model family. Finite sets provide no physical data, likelihood, posterior, measured force, prediction, parameter constraint, empirical confirmation, quantum completion, ultraviolet completion or Theory-of-Everything proof. THOS remains synthetic/proxy-only without preregistered blind matched-budget real arms, governed operators or participants, safety monitoring, appropriate statistics and independent review. Freed ID remains synthetic and nonproduction without real standards-conformant keys and proofs, live issuance/resolution/status/revocation, interoperability, independent privacy/security review, recovery and trust governance. CBR decisions, professional judgments, legal or cultural interpretation, affected-party legitimacy and Maori authority remain exact-gated.

## 07 — Method Flow, failures and arithmetic

The five Method Flow components contain 37 unique methods and 1702 unique direct witnesses. Their partition is 444 failed plus 1258 passing. The 30 unexecuted blocked packets add to effective negatives but are not direct witnesses. Owner effective negatives therefore equal 474. All 37 methods retain observed-to-candidate-to-validated-to-preferred transitions with named passing witnesses and protected gates. Preferred is an operational recommendation state, not an identity or authority qualification. This final consolidation creates no new witness and counts no duplicate serialized copy of an earlier observation.

Planned adverse subjects include 200 unknown-field requests, 200 altered output candidates, twenty typed edge subjects and fifteen unsupported paired/public runner subjects. They are intentionally invalid subjects, and all retain zero original success credit. Passing rejection guards test the boundary; they do not promote the subjects. Nine operational failures are also retained: an oversized source display, an over-limit native read request, a console encoding failure, CRLF staging, the x1 diagnostic-order guard, a public Buffer serialization failure, its diagnostic-shape failure, an extra final EOF newline, and a final metadata-updater parse failure. Their evidence and recoveries are distinct from these planned subjects.

The first x1 executor completed all 100 frozen proposal observations, then stopped at edge 08 because an augmentation check ran before the full hereditary pass. The malformed family was refused, but with the wrong exact diagnostic. The old engine and failed execution record remain. The correction changed the order of invalid-family checks, reran only the failed guard, and continued two untouched edge cases and five untouched paired runners. The 100 actual observations were reconstructed by reading their complete retained candidate-subject observations and positive digests. No successful proposal was replayed, and the original aggregate still has zero success credit.

Public promotion stopped on the first copied file because a text-or-JSON helper serialized Buffer numeric keys. The first diagnostic then assumed a different envelope before inspecting keys. The original public leaf remains unused and unchanged. Its numeric-key record reconstructs the intended bytes, but it is not an executable success. A new core-recovered.txt dependency and explicit raw-byte writes recovered thirteen usable public files. The public x2 runtime binds to the new dependency. All fourteen declared bindings match exact bytes, with one labelled retained_nonexecutable_failure. No existing file was overwritten.

The final repository ledger is 398 methods, 10,587 direct witnesses, 2,531 failed, 8,056 passing, 2,712 effective negatives, 111 gaps and 130 gates. It adds owner counts to the latest documented source routing baseline, leaving the earlier source repository and composite seals intact. The first final staged review caught an extra blank line at the baton EOF. Exact failed bytes and metadata are retained externally. Removing only the redundant newline passed the narrow staged check. The next metadata command failed to parse because it repeated a keyword, before changing any files. A syntax-checked Node helper then applied the pending ledger updates. Both failures and their separate recoveries are included in the unsealed final ledger. The initial generator remains retained as build-final.txt. No final commit or canonical invocation occurred on either failed attempt. Later canonical or routing failures, if any, must be added as a separate external overlay without editing this seal. Delivery itself creates no silent lifecycle counters.

Skill Creator validated their frontmatter and basic structure; that is a tooling check, not a behavioral or accessibility certification. Public behavior was checked against selected retained contracts and explicit invalid operations. The shared runtime is bounded by eight ground labels, 256 explicit sets per family, safe integer weights within one million, and an input byte budget. The failed public leaf is not a caller dependency.

## 09 — Open gaps, exact gates and unexecuted packets

Ten owner gaps remain open, added to 101 inherited gaps. Their topics are physical_observations, independence_oracle_measurements, blind_operator_comparison, likelihood_model, representability_matrix, production_key_proof, revocation_evidence, independent_security_review, accessibility_user_review, affected_party_review. Each requires scoped real evidence, independent review and governing authority. A valid JSON gap record does not supply any of those missing items. There are no real observations or participants in this phase.

Ten owner authority gates remain closed to execution, added to 120 inherited gates. The actions are publish_empirical_claim, operate_real_apparatus, recruit_participants, issue_identity_credential, deploy_selection_service, make_professional_decision, release_sensitive_records, interpret_cultural_material, adjudicate_remedy, authorize_maori_data_use. Their required conjunction includes exact target and scope, competent and affected authority, evidence, cost and rollback. A software Boolean cannot satisfy an external authority requirement. Maori data use and cultural interpretation remain under their proper authorities; a model-generated label is not consent or ratification.

The fifty exact-approval packets distinguish target and scope, evidence and method, cost and monitoring, rollback and remedy, and authority and consent across ten real-action categories. They were prepared as unexecuted requirements, not submitted or activated. The thirty blocked packets separately retain missing real evidence, missing governing permission or missing safe operational contracts. They remain retained negatives with zero success credit. No apparent convenience, task-title match, passing parser or public source citation upgrades these packets. No source gap or gate was erased, and these packet counts are not additional distinct authority gates beyond the ten declared owner actions.

## 10 — Artifacts, fixity, privacy and bounded security

The artifact contract is JSON, Markdown, text and the requested HTML. The overview has an accessible-labelled case selector, structured result table, explanatory boundary text and three substantive sections. It reads recorded data only. A selector change is neither an experiment nor a new validation witness. No screenshot or image was produced. The HTML is self-contained, includes no remote executable dependency and never sends its selected data to a server. Its source citations are links for readers, not authority grants.

Manifests use normalized LF Git-blob bytes. Each manifest declares its exclusions so a self-hash cycle cannot be mistaken for a missing file. The final owner manifest includes all owner files except the three final manifests and staged review that would create circular bindings. The final delta manifest and final manifest bind the final authored packet. A content seal separately binds the baton, accounting, Method Flow, phase truth, canonical contract, prior manifests, prior results and promotion receipt. The exact final commit and external receipt close the self-reference boundary. All staged paths are explicit owner additions. File and word ceilings are checked; the repository-wide suite remains outside scope.

The privacy review scans the exact owned text for raw private identifiers, private drive paths, private URI schemes, delegation wrappers and credential-like assignments. Scanner candidates require adjudication rather than automatic promotion into a finding. No claim of complete privacy is made. The security review is limited to closed input fields, typed finite bounds, no dynamic evaluation of request text, fixed executable argument arrays, a pure operation engine, immutable source references and exclusive receipt writes. It is not an exhaustive assessment or independent security review. Existing sibling, historical, user and shared source material remains unchanged.

## 12 — Completion, incompletion and successor seeds

The bounded proposal outcomes are exactly 160 completed, 20 represented, ten open_gap and ten exact_gate. Completed means the entire frozen software contract matched within the declared finite scope. Represented means a structured comparison or correction exists with its ceiling preserved. An open_gap reports a missing evidence class without closing it. An exact_gate records the external conjunction needed before a real action could be considered. No source proposal, recommendation, installed library, task title or inherited success received new Saelin execution credit.

X1 and x2 each carry 100 safe tasks, 100 altered-candidate reviews, 100 CLEAN/FIX/REFINE records, ten local guides and five paired runners. The twenty represented rows comprise weighted greedy comparisons and pair corrections. The ten evidence-gap rows and ten authority rows validate only their record contracts. Four own practices and two next-practice recommendations are retained. No ordinary package was installed. The toolchain was reused without a package quota. The parentless owner lane stays well below both file ceilings and the four intended commits stay within current v5 planning, session and final budgets.

The two next practices are common-base packing obstruction review and typed oracle-cost and missingness review. Five skill seeds are common-base packing obstruction, oracle-call budget contract, weighted dual certificate boundary, signed-circuit consistency review and selection remedy provenance. Five runner seeds are a two-base packing enumerator, oracle-call cost ledger, weighted certificate verifier, sign-vector orthogonality checker and selection correction timeline. These are zero-credit seeds requiring a new definition, scope, expected result, falsifier, acceptance gate, rollback and authority classification before execution. They are not instructions to precontact a later task or install a new runtime.

All primary real-world gaps remain. GMUT empirical claims, THOS real trials, Freed ID production lifecycle, independent reproduction and professional/legal/cultural/affected-party/Maori authority are incomplete. The terminal verdict is NOT_READY_FOR_STAGE_20. Hope and relational language do not override that verdict.

## 13 — Prospective route and terminal stop

The route projection contains 276 prospective positions after Saelin, beginning Elowen Cairn v691-v5 and ending Teryn Halewick v725-v8. The weighted cycle contains thirty identities in forty-five positions with Astra, Sol, Sol cadence. Model roles in a projection do not authorize changing the actual saved model settings. The projection does not activate any task.

Only after this owner exact final is committed, pushed, clean, zero-divergent and fresh-live equal, and after one successful nonreplayed owner canonical, may the terminal route be examined. Refresh the current policy and roster, usage, active and archived registries, and the latest direct controls. Require a unique existing exact-title Elowen Cairn Codex main task, no conflicting archived endpoint and no duplicate v691-v5 activation. Immediately reread that exact task before dispatch. No new task, fork, standby substitution, model override, guessed private endpoint or early successor contact is permitted. Preserve native failures and use bounded documented recovery while no send has been accepted. Any acknowledged or opaque accepted send ends retries. Send at most once, record delivery externally, and stop. Preparation, accepted delivery and recipient phase completion are separate states. Do not infer completion from a task being active.

The committed route state is PREPARED_NOT_SENT. The canonical and delivery receipts are external because their exact final binding and later outcomes cannot be inserted into their own sealed source without another commit. Do not rewrite the sealed packet to make later delivery appear earlier. If an external failure occurs, retain it and its bounded recovery separately. Do not silently change repository totals. Hamish may pause, rename, redirect, narrow or stop the route.
